ParlaMint-PL_2019-08-30-sejm-85-3.u368	Thank you very much. Dear Marshal! High Izbo! I am pleased to present to you a draft law amending the Act on Public Offering and the Conditions of Introduction of Financial Instruments to Organized Trading and Public Companies and some other laws. The main objective of the project is to ensure the application and implementation of European Union law on the capital market. The project also provides for a number of changes to observe developments in the domestic capital market. In view of the implementation in the national legal order of Directive 2017/828 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 17 May 2017 amending Directive 2007/36/EC as regards encouraging shareholders to engage in long-term activities, the project provides in particular: to facilitate companies whose shares are admitted to trading on a regulated market, to identify their shareholders and to communicate between those companies and their shareholders, to require asset managers to develop and make public their public participation policy, and to introduce an obligation for companies whose shares are admitted to trading on a regulated market, to establish remuneration policies for members of the Management Board and the Supervisory Board and to draw up periodic reports on the implementation of that policy. The changes presented should increase the involvement of shareholders of companies whose shares are admitted to trading on a regulated market, in those companies, in particular in the long term, among others. as a result of facilitating the exercise by shareholders of voting rights at general meetings. In addition, these changes should increase transparency for institutional investors and asset managers when investing in public companies. In turn, in order to ensure the application of Regulation 2017/1129 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 14 June 2017 on a prospectus to be published in connection with the offer to the public of securities or the admission to trading on a regulated market, and repealing Directive 2003/71/EC, the draft provides in particular for the designation of the Financial Supervision Commission as the competent authority for the purpose of monitoring compliance with the provisions of that Regulation, including the provision of appropriate supervisory and sanctioning powers. At the same time, the proposal provides for a number of regulatory changes to be applied in the light of the fact that the provisions of this Regulation are directly applicable. These changes should contribute to the harmonisation and simplification of prospectusing requirements in relation to offers of public securities, in particular for small and medium-sized enterprises. Regardless of ensuring compliance of Polish law with European Union law, the project provides in particular for the supervision of the Financial Supervision Commission so-called. ongoing offers of public securities, i.e. any subsequent offer not covered by the obligation to draw up a prospectus and made by the same issuer where the number of persons to whom it is addressed, together with the number of persons to whom offers of the same type of securities were addressed during the previous 12 months, exceed 149. The issuer making such tenders will be required to draw up an information memorandum and submit it for approval by the Financial Supervision Authority. In addition, it is about enabling the merger of investment funds of closed private assets. The proposed amendments to the rolling offer should prevent issuers from circumvention of the provisions on drawing up information documents, thereby strengthening the protection of the interests of non-professional investors. In turn, allowing investment funds to merge closed non-public assets will improve the management of closed national investment funds and reduce on the basis of the scale of the management costs of these entities. The bill submitted was the subject of extensive and long-term public consultation with the capital market environment, including the opinion of the Financial Supervision Commission and the National Bank of Poland. It is also important that the entry into force of the proposed law will not have a significant impact on the revenue and expenditure of the public finance sector. I would also like to point out that, although the project submitted is extensive, because it is contained on over 150 pages and provides for amendments to 23 laws, in a large part these are the resulting changes dictated by a change of name, i.e. "Prospectus of issue' to "prospect' or a change in the definition of a public company. High Izbo! In support of the bill submitted, I would like to express my conviction that the adoption of this law will contribute to increasing the efficiency of the national capital market, improving the level of investor protection and increasing the share of the capital market in financing the real economy, in particular small and medium-sized enterprises, and, of great importance, to reducing the risk of future distortions in the markets of financial instruments. Thank you very much.

The reasons for the bill will be presented by the Undersecretary of State at the Ministry of Finance, Mr Tomasz Kościński. You're welcome.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-08-30-sejm-85-3.u380	Dear Marshal! Ladies and gentlemen! The draft law amending the law on enforcement proceedings in the administration and certain other laws, Parliamentary printing No 3753, introduces significant amendments to the law on enforcement proceedings in the administration. This law regulates the principles and modalities of tax enforcement, as well as other public-law claims constituting the income of the state budget and local government units. We propose changes to improve administrative execution and efficiency. The proposed solutions are expected to increase the revenue of the state budget by more than PLN 732 million per year and the budgets of local government units. The proposed regulations provide for the simplification and orderly handling of appeals. The draft also provides for the separation of the tasks of the creditor and the enforcement authority in this respect. The changes will eliminate the existing duplication of enforcement bodies and creditors. The draft law governs the execution of the joint assets of the debtor and his spouse when the spouse of the debtor is responsible for the tax arrears of the joint assets. Such a solution will benefit the spouse not only because of the granting of the means of appeal but also because of the lower costs of execution. The bill improves proceedings in the event of an execution. In addition, the proposed provisions exempt enforcement authorities from the obligation to send copies of implementing titles and other documents to the executing authority for the combined execution. Instead of these documents, only one electronic document containing the necessary data will be transmitted. The bill facilitates and improves the execution of mobile devices. It introduces a new form of sales of movable property by the obliged party with the consent of the enforcement authority. Such a solution will speed up the execution of movable property and reduce the costs of running it. The draft law introduces the possibility to pay through payment terminals or to the account of the enforcement authority. The bill also improves the enforcement of overpayments or tax refunds, enabling electronic activities to be carried out. This will speed up the implementation of this enforcement measure and reduce the burden on the obliged person. In addition, the rules on money execution are amended. Until now, the provisions of the Enforcement Act in the administration have provided for no other form of execution from money than cash collection. The proposed changes will enable both enforcement authorities and creditors - in case of voluntary payment by the obliged party - to benefit from other solutions, e.g. payment terminals. The proposed law introduces the electronicisation of executions from accounts maintained by cooperative savings and credit banks and from the rights of financial instruments recorded on securities or other accounts. Electronicisation will make the enforcement measures more efficient and less costly for the obliged person. We want to introduce the principle of electronic transmission of implementing titles. Currently, according to the National Tax Administration data, only about 40% are transmitted by electronic means. implementing titles. A large number of paper-based implementing titles require manual data entry into the IT system. The solutions of the proposed law will enable automatic verification of data and their automatic registration in the electronic system of the enforcement body. The proposed regulation improves the implementation of safeguards on the basis of provisions on asset protection issued under the Code of Criminal Procedure and the Tax Criminal Code. The authority issuing the security shall not deal with any appeal. The proposed law amends the rules on the order of satisfaction of customs duties. Customs duties shall be met before enforcement costs charged in the execution of those duties. The need for a change in this regard is due to the European Commission's questioning of the applicable method of allocating the amount obtained from the execution. In view of the need to adapt the information systems - enforcement authorities and creditors - to the proposed solutions, the law will enter into force 9 months after the date of publication, with the exception of: a modification of the Customs Law, which enters into force on 1 January 2020, changes in the enforcement of bank accounts and accounts maintained by the SKOK, which enter into force 12 months after the date of publication, the provisions relating to the electronic form of further and subsequent implementing title, which enter into force 18 months after the date of publication, the electronicisation of the enforcement of financial instruments, which shall enter into force 24 months after the date of publication. In conclusion, the bill provides for a number of improvements that will affect the effectiveness of executions while at the same time ensuring full protection of the rights of the obliged party. In presenting the above, I propose that the submitted project be submitted for further work. Thank you very much.

Using experience to date in the application of rules, which give rise to a lot of ambiguities, are too complicated, modifications have been made, among others. certain enforcement measures, the rules on the handling of appeals have been amended, the powers of enforcement authorities and creditors have been allocated. I ask the Undersecretary of State in the Ministry of Justice of Mr Marcin Romanowski to present the justification for the bill. You're welcome.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-08-30-sejm-85-3.u390	Dear Marshal! High Izbo! The draft law amending the Code of Administrative Procedure and certain other laws aims to enable the issuing of electronic certificates bearing electronic stamp and to enable courts, prosecutors, police, special services to obtain information from the National Criminal Register by means of network services, which will significantly improve the procedures for issuing certificates from the KRK, as well as certificates issued by other public authorities. Currently, information from the National Criminal Register, regardless of how it is awarded, shall constitute certificates within the meaning of Chapter VII of the Code of Administrative Procedure. On the other hand, according to the provision of the article. § 217 § 4 k.p.a. the certificate shall be issued in the form of an electronic document bearing a qualified electronic signature if the applicant so requests. In addition, it is possible to issue a certificate in a traditional form, i.e. paper form. Therefore, it is not possible in the current legal state to issue certificates fully automated by public administrations. In any case, the certificate must be signed by the person authorised - either manually or electronically, depending on the form of the certificate chosen. This requirement significantly extends the procedure for issuing a certificate, which may adversely affect the interests of persons or entities wishing to obtain a certificate. However, in many cases the issue of a certificate does not require human intervention and can be as simplified as possible. The certificate is an act of knowledge of the administrative authority about a certain factual or legal condition, and these states are in practice in most cases simple. The requirement to provide in any case a certificate with a signature assigned to a natural person - a worker of the authority authorised to issue certificates - is therefore too restrictive. It is necessary to provide for the possibility of issuing certificates bearing a qualified electronic stamp by public administrations. The draft law also provides for the introduction of a new channel for the provision of information from the National Criminal Register: a network service that will enable certain entities to obtain information from the KRK through their own systems. Both of the above mentioned functionalities, i.e. electronic seal and network services, will allow for the automation of the process of providing information from the National Criminal Register in real time. New functionalities of the KRK system, which were built as part of the project of building the information system of the National Criminal Register together with organisational and legislative changes, so-called. KRK 2.0 is urgently needed, among others. courts and law enforcement authorities, as they will contribute to speeding up their tasks and their proceedings. The National Criminal Register is a key state register and part of the country's critical infrastructure. It is used by millions of citizens of the Republic of Poland, citizens of the Member States of the European Union and third-country nationals. Rules are constantly coming in to determine the need to determine that a person is unpunished. Each year about 1.5 million applications of this type are submitted to the KRK. The KRK participates in the European Criminal Records Information System, ECRIS. The KRK shall also provide information to countries outside the European Union on the basis of international agreements. The quality and timeliness of this information influences the efficiency of procedures and the participation of Poland in ensuring security in Europe and the world. Therefore, I ask the High House to support this proposal. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

I'm opening a discussion. Mr Andrzej Matusiewicz will be the first to speak on behalf of the Law and Justice Club. You're welcome.
ParlaMint-PL_2019-08-30-sejm-85-3.u392	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, Law and Justice, I present my position on the draft law amending the Code of Administrative Procedure and certain other laws, the printing of Seym No 3669. Currently, information about the person and information about the collective entity from the National Criminal Register, regardless of the manner in which they are granted, shall constitute certificates within the meaning of Chapter VII of the Code of Administrative Procedure. In accordance with the provision of the Article. 217 § 4 k.p.a., which was amended in 2000, the certificate shall be issued in the form of an electronic document bearing a qualified electronic signature if the applicant so requests - at the request of the person concerned. It is also possible, of course, to issue a certificate in a traditional form. Therefore, it is not possible in the current legal state to issue certificates fully automated by public administrations. Recently, many actors, especially those involved in justice, such as courts, prosecutors' offices, special services - as well as citizens - have applied for a certificate of non-punishment to the National Criminal Register. According to the information contained in the explanatory memorandum of the bill, 1,300 thousand have a yearly impact. Ask questions. The Minister spoke about 1500 thousand in his speech. Maybe so. It's growing, avalanchely. These certificates will be issued using special solutions. The procedure is to be simplified in such a way that it will be possible to issue certificates with a qualified electronic stamp and will be the entire electronic system for issuing them through the use of a network service, so-called. web service. This is intended to simplify the procedure. There is also a major change in the provisions of the National Criminal Register Act. Actually, that's what caused it. amendment of the Article. § 217 § 4 k.p.a. 80% The questions to the KRK are addressed by courts, prosecutors, police and special forces. The head of the National Criminal Information Centre will also be able to receive information from the National Criminal Register using a network service, as will the Border Guard and Military Intelligence Service. The Act is expected to enter into force on 1 October 2019. The project was positively assessed by the Supreme Court, the National Court of Justice and the Public Prosecutor General of the Republic of Poland. On behalf of the Law and Justice Club, I request that this project be supported by the High House and that it be given a further legislative run by referral to the Extraordinary Commission for changes in codification. This leads to a significant extension of the procedure for issuing the certificate. The draft submitted aims to change this situation by introducing in the Code of Administrative Procedure the possibility of issuing certificates with a qualified electronic stamp. This will allow the certification process to be fully automated and as short as possible. These changes are quite simple and, I believe, do not cause controversy, but at the same time will be extremely useful for people and entities who want to be certified as soon as possible. ParlaMint-PL_2019-08-30-sejm-85-3.u402	Thank you very much. Dear Marshal! High Izbo! I'll answer the first question. The system meets all cybersecurity requirements. I'm answering the second question. However, following the introduction of these solutions, both legislative and technological - because these are the certificates issued for those entities which are listed in the catalogue, mainly the courts, prosecutors and police offices - about 80% these certificates will be issued in real time. Thus, this will also automatically speed up the issuing of certificates for those entities which do not have this technical possibility. So this is the acceleration. We're talking about speeding up the speed of obtaining information about non-punishment, above all by the courts, the prosecution and the police. With regard to the deadline of 30 March, it is linked to the fact that the various entities indicated in the Act which obtain the possibility of receiving certificates by means of a web service will at different times be able to adapt their ICT infrastructure to these legal possibilities. At the same time, we have created a solution that will enable those who already adapt, for example. first of all, the courts, which will be mostly ready to obtain these certificates at the time of the entry into force of the Act, so that they can use them immediately. But it is precisely this term for the listed enumeratively in the proposed regulations of entities that is just 30 March. It follows from the arrangements between these actors that all these entities will be able to adapt their ICT systems at that time. Thank you very much.

We proceed with the examination of item 47 of the agenda: the draft resolution presented by the Sejm Bureau on the commemoration of the 80th anniversary of the outbreak of the Second World War (print No 3800). Ladies and gentlemen, please rise. [[The Gathered Rise Up]] The resolution of the Polish Sejm on commemorating the 80th anniversary of the outbreak of the Second World War. September 1, 1939, at 1 p.m. 4.40 German aviation attacked Wieluń without warning. A few minutes later, the German training armadillo 'Schleswig-Holstein' fired on the Polish military depot at Westerplatte in Gdańsk. These events, together with the declaration of war by the Germans by England and France and the attack of the Soviet Union on Poland on 17 September 1939, began World War II. It was the largest world war in history, which lasted from September 1, 1939 to September 2, 1945, and in Europe until May 8, 1945. War activities covered almost the whole of Europe, eastern and south-eastern Asia, northern Africa, parts of the Middle East and all oceans. Over 1.5 billion people participated in the war, including over 100 million people with guns. According to various estimates, 50 to 78 million people were killed. World War II has caused man's suffering. Poland was a country where the scale of material damage, in the field of cultural goods, but also in the social structure was huge. Poles were subjected to numerous repressions, such as executions, resettlements, and property collections. This was done by both Nazi Germany and the Soviet Union. In the occupied territory of Poland, Germany established concentration camps - places where the mass extermination of the Jewish people, but also Poles and many other nations, was carried out on an unprecedented scale. Poles fought against enemies in the September Campaign in defence of their Homeland, and later on almost all fronts of the world. During the German and Soviet occupation they built structures of the Polish Underground State and took arms to free themselves from invaders. In memory of the generations, as a symbol of the Polish pursuit of freedom, the Warsaw Uprising, which broke out on 1 August 1944, will remain. The Sejm of the Republic of Poland on the 80th anniversary of the outbreak of the Second World War honors the memory of the defenders of the Homeland and all soldiers and civilians who died and murdered in the years 1939-1945. Please accept this resolution by acclamation. [[Applause]] I state that the Sejm has adopted a resolution on commemorating the 80th anniversary of the outbreak of the Second World War. The aim of KPN was to regain independence by Poland through the removal of Soviet domination, the liquidation of the power of the PZPR, the restoration of democracy and the holding of free elections. KPN activists paid for their actions with prisons, repressions, and some also with life. ParlaMint-PL_2019-08-30-sejm-85-3.u472	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I wanted to raise an important problem, in our country relatively little known. It concerns electrosmog. Electrosmog is an electromagnetic smog. This is the definition of electromagnetic radiation of different frequencies, belonging to the range of non-ionizing radiation, most often referring to a fragment of the range of radio waves from 300 kHz to 300 GHz. They are generated by radio, television, mobile phone, alarm, wireless Internet, Wi-Fi and Bluetooth. There are scientific studies that clearly prove that the electromagnetic field affects many diseases, including cancer. There are people among us with hypersensitivity to the electromagnetic field. On a macro scale, this field is made up of base antennas, on a micro scale - cell phones. According to statistics from the World Health Organization, WHO, human health is getting worse and worse. WHO says electrosmog contributes to this. And it helps by disrupting our sleep, reducing our efficiency. It causes fatigue, fatigue, even depression, headaches, migraine, tinnitus, burnout syndrome, dizziness, cardiac disorders, blood pressure disorders - both up and down, i.e. pressure too low and too high - anxiety, tingling and numbness of the limbs, problems with learning in children, arguing, ADHD and hyperactivity in children. It's just a small part of what the WHO said. We can now purchase meters, similar to smog, for electrosmog testing and determine if we are dealing with a field within the normal range under the conditions in which we live and live. Each of these standards shows these standards. This is an electrical standard and an electromagnetic standard. I will read at the end of the recommendation of the Cypriot National Committee on the Environment and Health of Children, based on French, Israeli and many other countries' recommendations. We don't have that recommendation. These are the following: protect children and pregnant women from the potential effects of radiation emitted by wireless devices, such as mobile phones and electronic babysitting, and during Internet calls via Wi-Fi or mobile data. Wi-Fi connection takes place between the router and the mobile device - smartphone, tablet or laptop - with Wi-Fi enabled. Mobile data is an unfortunate conspiracy. It is about data transmission between a mobile phone base station and a mobile device - a smartphone, tablet or laptop - with mobile data enabled. The Bluetooth connection takes place between the computer or mobile phone and its accessories: wireless keyboard, mouse or headphone. Non-ionizing radiation emitted during communication connections, in the frequency range from 30 kHz to 300GHz, is potentially harmful to humans. I call on my parents to limit the use of mobile phones, electronic devices, to create blockages for so-called. hanging on the Internet. ParlaMint-PL_2019-08-30-sejm-85-3.u480	Thank you. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Thanks to the historical policy pursued by the Government of Law and Justice on 1 September 1939, the date of the start of World War II is becoming increasingly important in the world space. August 27th this year The Brazilian Parliament commemorated this anniversary with a solemn session and a minute of silence. This is not a trivial event, for example, because Europe's countries often associated the date of the outbreak of World War II with the date of the attack on their country. The anniversary of the defensive war of 1939 is a command to remember Polish victims and losses, also about unfulfilled plans and dreams not included in statistics. Evil is also the sum of individual fates, which as elements of the puzzle form a historical picture of the Polish society. As an envoy from Lublin, I will mention the sick. Wincenty Tomasiewicz from Nurzyna near Łukow, inventor of a dive rescue apparatus and commander of ORP He was killed on September 1, along with 17 sailors on the ship during the German raid on the oxywki port. So far, in the public space of the Polish Coast there is no mention of this event, even in the form of a modest plaque. I will mention Mr Władysław Aleksander Górski, a farmer from Sętek near Łukowa, a village of Ulan, who, as one of the few, arrived at the meeting of the Polish Sejm on 2 September 1939. He was later imprisoned by the Germans and repressed by the Communists. Jan Gilas was the janitor of the Lublin magistrate in September 1939 when a bomb dropped by a German aircraft fell into the town hall building. He threw himself into an explosion to protect the people there. He died of heart disease on the steps of the city hall. His picture, including a big bomb with his hands, is dramatic in its expression. At the same time, the soldier and inventor, the messenger, the janitor of the magistrate - all of them, and the whole nation, united the community of fate. In shaping today's community of Poles, let us remember those who created it with their lives. Thank you for your attention.

In Czokpaku join the troops that were organized and left the Soviet Union with General Anders. Ladies and gentlemen, this military act was occupied by the blood of many Poles. In the group of these people was my father, Captain. Antoni Badowski, who left this world two years ago, but remembered this military act and gave me this information. Anthony Badowski, and a lot of other soldiers whose names I can't remember. There's only four of them left right now. I wanted to honor and remember these soldiers - those who died in the field of glory, those who left for natural causes, as well as those living four veterans. Hello to their memory.

ParlaMint-PL_2019-08-30-sejm-85-3.u488	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! The fight to keep the gold is still going on. When Poland entered the European Union, various issues related to it were raised. Even then, before the referendum took place in the Polish Parliament, I raised the question of defending Poland's right to conduct its own monetary policy. Before Poland entered the European Union, many economists spoke, with the then president of the NBP Leszek Balcerowicz, in which we were encouraged to liquidate the gold. I'll admit the fight was amazing. Many times and in various ways during the meetings of the Polish Parliament and the Committee on Public Finance, I have tried to present arguments for the maintenance of the gold and against the entry of Poland into the euro area in cooperation with other Members who understand the issue. I perfectly remember the debate on 5 November 2004. At that time Minister of Finance in the Government of SLD Mirosław Gronicki encouraged us to liquidate the gold. I was one of those people who spoke directly against Poland's entry into the euro area. What's more, I already presented an alternative programme at that time. Its essence was to make better use of the fact that we have our own currency and our own central bank. I remember the moods from 2001, 2002, 2003. At that time, the mainstream media was dominated by economists who supported the liquidation of the gold. Research conducted by the Polish Bank Association showed that the vast majority of bankers were for the euro and against the gold. The murderous organic work in the Polish Sejm, publications in the pages of Our Journal, as well as broadcasts in the context of the unfinished talks on TV Trwam and Radio Maryja managed to make many groups of Poles aware of how important it was, has and will have gold. I also defended the Polish nature of the Warsaw Stock Exchange, thinking that it should not be sold in the hands of a foreign strategic investor. He supported me in this, among other things. sleep. Marian Wronierski, a member of the authorities of the Union of Brokers and Advisors, who understood well that the Polish economist should not only have education and expertise, but should also have strong patriotic motivation. This hard fight to keep the gold was very strong for me. This enabled us to inoculate arguments for keeping the gold even where it seems to be extremely difficult. The results of research conducted by the Polish Bank Association have even started showing that Polish bankers are not in favour of the euro but of the gold. This change in the mood of Polish bankers is a huge success. Moreover, even from the latest survey on the subject, bankers themselves admit that it would be detrimental for households to enter the euro area. On the other hand, the bankers surveyed admit that they believe that only large multinational corporations could benefit from the liquidation of the gold. For us, on the other hand, the welfare of all Poles, the well-being of citizens, ordinary consumers is crucial. In addition, it is still and very consistently important to stress that, having our own currency, we can set the actual objectives of the central bank's activities ourselves, we can decide on monetary policy instruments and on the central bank's position in the structure of the various national authorities. That's why the next election will be so important. It is extremely important to bet on those people who have proven with their attitude of life that they understand the importance of their currency for Poland. It is both a symbol - the images of Polish kings appear on the banknotes - as well as an important attribute of economic sovereignty, which must not be disposed of. The fact that the Polish economy has been developing rapidly in recent years and unemployment is also a result of the fact that, having its own central bank, we can conduct monetary policy so that it is as beneficial as possible for the Polish economy. I promise that if I am elected for the next term of office of the Polish Parliament, I will do everything in my power to bring back to the public debate strong arguments in favour of maintaining our national currency, and also - what this entails - the need to defend and promote what is Polish in the economic sphere, for the need to defend the Polish character of the most important economic operators operating in our country, for the need to further recolonise the media, trade and banks and for the need to maintain the Warsaw Stock Exchange in Polish hands and implement such a strategy for the development of the Polish capital market in order to serve the development of our economy as much as possible. Thank you very much.

It is to them, Polish children of war, that we dedicate this action. They will tell us about this Polish children of war, and we, knowing that this is the last generation of living witnesses, will inspire our ears, record memories, warm our hearts and emotions. I encourage you, Polish, to take part in the September march. It's also your personal business, wandering into memories you've ignored so far, your grandmother or grandpa. So make good tea, put prunes cake, after all season, and listen to the story, and learn life after all, contrary to everything, sticking to the line at the end of which there is always faith, hope and love.
